# Digital Product - A Premium Notion Finance Dashboard Research Brief (for review)

Status: planned
Audience: Operator
Output goal: human-friendly PDF or approval pack

## Original Instruction

Evaluate a premium Notion finance dashboard template and prepare a decision pack.

## Workflow

Digital Product - A Premium Notion Finance Dashboard Viability Pipeline

## Purpose

Human-friendly research brief covering target market, demand signals, competitors, risks, and recommendation.

## Planned Sections

- Executive summary
- Target customer
- Market signals
- Competitor notes
- Risks
- Recommendation

## Notes

This is a runtime-created deliverable shell. The agent runner will fill this with polished content, then convert important operator-facing outputs to PDF when the PDF generator is implemented.

## Goal and success criteria

Agent: orchestrator
Status: dry-run drafted

Turn the operator instruction into a controlled Digital Product validation workflow for A Premium Notion Finance Dashboard.

Evidence / checks:
- Goal has been reduced into research, commercial, risk, design, and QC tasks.
- Live external actions remain blocked until adapters, credentials, and approval rules are proven.

Next action: Run market research with a live research adapter when available; use dry-run assumptions until then.

## Market research dry-run

Agent: researcher
